Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The spectroscopy operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 744. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 129 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 744 + O(1/n^129). Summing over all modes and applying the spectroscopy convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
